TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于ALADIN框架的分布式共识优化算法C-ALADIN，包括集中式和去中心化两种变体，分别支持一阶和二阶变体，并针对有向图与量化通信场景进行了扩展，理论保证了凸问题的全局收敛性和非凸问题的局部收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布式共识优化方法在通信效率和收敛速度之间存在权衡，尤其是在非凸问题和去中心化场景下缺乏有效的快速收敛算法。ALADIN框架在集中式优化中表现出色，但尚未被充分扩展到共识优化场景。

Method: 1）提出C-ALADIN算法，扩展ALADIN框架以直接处理共识约束，引入中心协调器；2）设计一阶变体和采用Hessian近似的二阶变体，避免直接传输二阶信息；3）通过有限时间协调协议，提出在带量化通信的有向图上运行的去中心化版本。

Result: 凸问题具有全局收敛性，非凸问题具有局部收敛性；去中心化版本的迭代收敛到由量化水平决定的最优解邻域内；数值实验表明所提方法在保持快速收敛的同时显著降低了通信和计算成本。

Conclusion: C-ALADIN及其去中心化变体在分布式共识优化中实现了收敛速度与通信/计算效率的良好平衡，为大规模分布式优化提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Distributed optimization has found widespread applications in smart grids, optimal control, and machine learning. This paper studies distributed consensus optimization. We extend the Augmented Lagrangian-based Alternating Direction Inexact Newton (ALADIN) framework to propose Consensus ALADIN (C-ALADIN) with a central coordinator, which directly handles consensus constraints. Our C-ALADIN algorithm admits both a first-order variant and a second-order variant that employs a Hessian approximation, avoiding direct transmission of second-order information while preserving fast local convergence. We then develop a decentralized version of C-ALADIN that operates over directed graphs with quantized communication, using a finite-time coordination protocol. For both versions, we establish global convergence guarantees for convex problems and local convergence guarantees for non-convex problems. For the decentralized case, the iterates converge to a neighborhood of the optimum determined by the quantization level. Numerical results demonstrate that our methods retain fast convergence while substantially reducing communication and computational costs compared to existing decentralized approaches.

</details>


### [72] [Time-Dependent PDE-Constrained Optimization via Weak-Form Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20639)
*April Tran,Terry Haut,David Bortz,Youngsoo Choi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出基于弱形式潜空间动力学识别（WLaSDI）的降阶建模框架，用于加速梯度驱动的含时PDE约束优化。该方法将高维解轨迹压缩到低维潜空间，利用弱形式系统识别学习参数化潜动力学，避免了训练轨迹的数值微分，提升了噪声鲁棒性。在热辐射传输、双流不稳定性Vlasov-Poisson系统和无粘Burgers方程三个基准问题上，WLaSDI实现了高达五个数量级的加速，并在含噪数据下保持优化精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维、含时的PDE约束优化问题需要反复求解正问题和灵敏度，全阶优化计算成本极高，亟需高效的降阶替代模型来加速优化过程。

Method: 基于WLaSDI框架：1) 将高维解轨迹压缩到低维潜空间；2) 利用弱形式系统识别（避免数值微分）学习参数化潜动力学；3) 推导直接灵敏度和伴随法梯度表达式，实现可扩展的梯度计算。

Result: 在三个含时基准问题上，WLaSDI框架获得准确的最优设计，在含噪训练数据下保持鲁棒性，计算加速比高达五个数量级。

Conclusion: 弱形式潜动力学为复杂含时PDE系统的梯度优化提供了高效且噪声鲁棒的替代模型基础。

Abstract: Optimization problems constrained by high-dimensional, time-dependent partial differential equations require repeated forward and sensitivity solves, making high-fidelity optimization computationally prohibitive in many-query design and control settings. We present a weak-form latent-space reduced-order modeling framework for accelerating gradient-based PDE-constrained optimization. The proposed approach builds on Weak-form Latent Space Dynamics Identification (WLaSDI), which compresses high-dimensional solution trajectories into a low-dimensional latent representation and identifies parametric latent dynamics using weak-form system identification. By avoiding explicit numerical differentiation of training trajectories, the weak-form improves robustness to noisy data and yields more reliable surrogate dynamics for optimization. We formulate the resulting reduced PDE-constrained optimization problem and derive both direct-sensitivity and adjoint-based gradient expressions for the learned latent dynamics, enabling scalable gradient evaluation with respect to design parameters. The framework is demonstrated on three time-dependent benchmark problems: thermal radiative transfer for optimal hohlraum design, the two-stream instability Vlasov-Poisson system, and the inviscid Burgers equation. Across these examples, WLaSDI produces accurate optimal designs, remains robust under noisy training data, and delivers substantial computational savings, including speedups of up to five orders of magnitude relative to full-order optimization. These results demonstrate that weak-form latent dynamics provide an efficient and noise-robust surrogate foundation for gradient-based optimization of complex time-dependent PDE systems.

</details>


### [73] [Policy Gradient for Continuous-Time Mean-Field Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20718)
*Erhan Bayraktar,Martin Hernandez,Qinxin Yan,Yuhua Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对折扣无限时域下的熵正则化平均场控制问题，提出了一种策略梯度方法，推导出无需额外求解方程的直接策略梯度公式，并构建了模型驱动的Actor-Critic算法，通过数值实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平均场控制的策略梯度方法在计算价值函数后，仍需额外求解方程才能获得策略梯度，计算效率较低。本文旨在推导一种直接基于价值函数的显式策略梯度公式，避免这一额外的求解步骤。

Method: 1) 考虑随机化反馈策略与耦合的代表性粒子-总体系统，其中代表性状态与由McKean-Vlasov方程描述的总体分布共同演化；2) 定义瞬时优势函数来量化当前随机化策略下采取特定动作的增益；3) 推导Gâteaux策略梯度公式，给出目标泛函沿任意策略扰动的一阶变分；4) 在有限维策略参数化下导出相应的上升方向；5) 构建模型驱动的Actor-Critic框架，Critic通过求解线性稳态HJB方程（使用柱函数表示对总体分布的依赖）得到，Actor根据策略梯度公式更新。

Result: 1) 得到了显式的策略梯度公式，无需额外求解方程；2) 证明了PDE在多项式增长函数类中的适定性；3) 在LQR模型和人群运动问题上通过数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的策略梯度方法为熵正则化平均场控制问题提供了一种计算高效的求解途径，其显式梯度公式简化了算法流程，数值实验展示了该方法在实际问题中的潜力。

Abstract: This paper develops a policy gradient method for entropy-regularized mean-field control in the discounted infinite-horizon setting. We consider randomized feedback policies and a coupled representative-particle/population system, in which the representative state evolves jointly with a population law governed by a McKean--Vlasov equation. The resulting value function is therefore defined on the product space $\mathbb R^d \times \mathcal P_2(\mathbb R^d)$.
  A key distinction from existing policy gradient methods for mean-field control is that, after computing the value function under a fixed policy, our approach does not require solving an additional equation to obtain the policy gradient. Instead, we derive an explicit policy gradient formula directly in terms of the value function. The formulation is based on an instantaneous advantage function, which quantifies the gain of taking a given action relative to the current randomized policy. We establish a Gâteaux policy-gradient formula, which gives the first-order variation of the objective along arbitrary policy perturbations, and then derive the corresponding ascent direction under finite-dimensional policy parametrization.
  The resulting formula leads to a model-based actor--critic scheme. The critic is obtained by solving the associated linear stationary Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman equation for the value function, using cylindrical functions to represent dependence on the population law. The actor is then updated according to the derived policy-gradient formula. We further analyze the well-posedness of the PDE in a polynomial-growth function class. Finally, we illustrate the proposed method through numerical experiments on an LQR model and a crowd-motion problem.

</details>

*Nicholas Pischke*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在Hadamard空间（非正曲率完备测地线度量空间）中，用于极小化凸积分函数的随机近端点法的几乎必然弱收敛，解决了M. Bačák提出的一个问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决M. Bačák提出的开放问题，将随机近端点法从强度量正则性条件或局部紧空间推广到更一般的非线性Hadamard空间框架下。

Method: 结合Hadamard空间中随机拟Fejér单调过程的几乎必然弱收敛定理与均值函数值几乎必然收敛到最小值的论证。

Result: 证明了在温和增长条件（推广Lipschitz连续性）下，随机近端点法在Hadamard空间中几乎必然弱收敛。

Conclusion: 成功将随机近端点法的收敛性分析推广到更一般的非线性Hadamard空间，弱化了先前所需的正则性条件。

Abstract: We prove the almost sure weak convergence of a stochastic proximal point method for minimizing a convex integral function in the general nonlinear context of complete geodesic metric spaces of nonpositive curvature (so-called Hadamard spaces), solving a problem of M. Bačák. This method, formulated in the context of a mild growth condition on the function which generalizes Lipschitz continuity, was previously only considered in the context of strong metric regularity conditions or in the context of locally compact spaces. The proof is a combination of a weak almost sure convergence theorem for stochastic processes in Hadamard spaces which confine to a stochastic variant of quasi-Fejér monotonicity, due to previous work of the author, together with a new argument for proving the almost sure convergence of the mean function values of the process towards the minimal value.

</details>


### [75] [Polynomial diagrams for microstructure modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20816)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幂图和各向异性幂图只能产生直线或二次曲线作为细胞边界，表达能力有限。本文旨在提出一个统一框架，将边界推广至任意指定次数的代数曲线，从而更灵活地拟合复杂图像数据（如材料微观结构）。

Method: 1）将幂图（一次）和各向异性幂图（二次）重新解释为线性参数化最小化图，自然推广至多项式图（任意次数）。2）采用勒让德多项式作为基函数。3）设计正则化凹目标函数（源自逻辑回归）。4）开发GPU加速优化算法。5）提供优化算法理论分析（尺度/规范不变性、正则化极限行为）。

Result: 成功将多项式图拟合到钢的电子背散射衍射（EBSD）图像，验证了框架的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 多项式图是一个灵活的框架，能够以代数曲线边界拟合图像数据，GPU加速使其适用于实际材料科学图像分析，理论分析保证了优化算法的可靠性。

Abstract: We formulate a framework of polynomial diagrams, which are a generalisation of power diagrams (PDs) and anisotropic power diagrams (APDs) allowing for boundaries between cells to be algebraic curves of a prescribed degree. We show that they arise naturally from rephrasing PDs (APDs) as first-degree (second-degree) instances of linear parametrised minimisation diagrams. We also develop an efficient GPU-accelerated framework for fitting polynomial diagrams to image data using Legendre polynomials and by maximising a regularised concave objective function adapted from classical logistic regression literature. A largely self-contained analysis of the optimisation algorithm is also provided, including identification of scale and gauge invariances and the limiting objective function as the regularisation parameter vanishes. We apply the algorithm to fit polynomial diagrams to electron backscatter diffraction images of steel.

</details>


### [76] [Large Language Models for Operations Research: A Comprehensive Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20849)
*Xianchao Xiu,Jianhao Li,Jun Fan,Wanquan Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文系统综述了大语言模型在运筹学中的应用（LLM4OR），涵盖模型构建、算法设计和解验证等角色，并总结了基准数据集和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运筹学方法依赖专家知识，难以高效解决大规模、多约束问题，而大语言模型的快速发展为解决这些挑战提供了新的研究范式。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法，首先介绍OR问题定义和LLM基本原理，聚焦分析LLM在OR中的三种角色：模型构建、算法设计、解验证，并讨论实际应用场景和基准数据集。

Result: 梳理了LLM4OR领域的现状，包括LLM在OR问题解决中的不同作用模式、代表性应用场景及公开基准数据集，并识别了当前面临的关键挑战。

Conclusion: LLM为运筹学提供了新的研究范式，但仍面临诸多挑战；未来研究方向包括进一步提升LLM在OR任务中的可靠性和效率。

Abstract: Operations Research (OR) serves as a core decision-support methodology for complex systems, with significant applications across mathematics, management science, and computer science. Traditional approaches heavily rely on expert knowledge and often struggle to efficiently solve large-scale and multi-constraint problems. The rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs) in recent years has offered a novel research paradigm to address these challenges. This paper presents a systematic survey of Large Language Models for Operations Research (LLM4OR). We begin by introducing the definition of OR problems and the fundamental principles of LLMs. We then focus on analyzing the roles of LLMs in OR, specifically covering such as model formulation, algorithm design, and solution verification. In addition, we discuss practical applications in representative scenarios and summarize benchmark datasets in this field. Finally, we outline the key challenges and provide perspectives on future research directions. A collection of related literature is available at https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/LLM4OR.

</details>


### [77] [Spare Strategy for Large-Scale Satellite Constellations Under Dual Resupply Channels Using Markov Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20875)
*Seungyeop Han,Shoji Yoshikawa,Takumi Noro,Takumi Suda,Koki Ho*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于马尔可夫链的混合备用卫星管理架构分析与设计方法，用于大规模星座的早期阶段设计。该方法混合了间接路径（通过重型火箭将备用卫星部署到停车轨道）和直接路径（通过小型运载火箭将备用卫星直接送入轨道平面），并通过遗传算法在成本最小化和弹性约束下进行优化。案例验证表明混合策略在特定条件下优于纯策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在分析大规模卫星星座的混合备用管理架构时存在聚合假设，无法准确捕捉轨道力学约束下的随机、多层级动态特性，且适用域有限。因此需要一种能够在早期设计阶段有效评估混合策略长期可行性的分析框架。

Method: 将卫星故障与补货过程建模为马尔可夫链：间接通道采用周期性盘点再订购点/订购量策略，直接通道采用标准再订购点/订购量策略。通过固定点迭代在升交点赤经周期上求解耦合链的周期稳态，并引入近似分析方法在保持延迟统计量的同时大幅降低模型规模。在此基础上构建带弹性约束的成本最小化问题，使用遗传算法求解。

Result: 通过蒙特卡洛仿真验证了分析方法的准确性；案例研究展示了框架的实际应用价值，能够识别混合策略优于纯策略的条件。

Conclusion: 该框架是通道中立的，优化过程能够自主选择最优路径及其角色，避免了先验假设。混合备用管理架构在特定条件下能够比纯策略（仅间接或仅直接路径）更优。

Abstract: This paper presents a Markov-chain-based method for the early-phase analysis and design of hybrid spare-management architectures for large-scale satellite constellations.} The hybrid strategy combines two channels: an indirect path that stages spares in parking orbits via heavy launch for later transfer to constellation planes, and a direct path that delivers spares to in-plane orbits using small launch vehicles. {To assess the long-run viability of such concepts of operations, satellite failure and replenishment processes are modeled as a Markov chain:} the indirect channel follows a periodic-review reorder-point/order-quantity policy, while the direct channel uses a standard reorder-point/order-quantity policy. These coupled chains yield a periodic steady state over the right ascension of the ascending node cycle via fixed-point iteration, and the stationary distributions provide rigorous cost and resilience metrics. By directly modeling the stochastic, multi-echelon dynamics governed by orbital mechanics, our framework avoids the aggregation assumptions of prior works and remains valid across a wider operating domain. We also introduce an approximate analysis that preserves delay statistics while significantly reducing model size. Building on this fast, accurate analysis, we formulate a cost minimization problem with resilience constraints and solve it using a genetic algorithm. The framework is channel-neutral; the optimization autonomously selects the preferred path and roles. {A case study validates the analysis against Monte Carlo simulations and demonstrates the practical value of the framework in identifying the conditions under which the hybrid policy outperforms pure strategies.

</details>


### [78] [Continuous-Time Analysis for Minimax and Bilevel Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20898)
*Hyunwoo Lee,Jeongyeol Kwon,Dohyun Kwon*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出首个用于嵌套优化连续时间分析的统一Lyapunov模板，覆盖极小极大、双层（通过 lifted 罚函数形式）和 min-min-max 问题，通过模块化引理统一表征时间尺度分离，并从强凸/凹推广到仅凸（通过误差界条件），给出显式闭式阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌套优化（如极小极大、双层优化）的连续时间分析通常依赖于针对具体问题和条件构造的Lyapunov函数，缺乏统一的框架。

Method: 提出统一的Lyapunov模板；通过模块化可复用引理给出时间尺度分离的统一表征；引入误差界条件将分析从强凸/凹推广到仅凸情形；推导显式闭式阈值；将罚函数动力学与理想超梯度流比较，给出有限时间跟踪界，并讨论Euler一步离散化。

Result: 得到了统一的Lyapunov分析框架、显式闭式阈值（避免了离散时间分析中常见的耦合比率条件）、有限时间跟踪界，并通过超清洁诊断实验验证了预测的相对时间尺度区域在前向Euler离散化下依然可见。

Conclusion: 该统一模板为嵌套优化的连续时间动力学提供了通用分析工具，并揭示了离散化后理论预测与数值行为的一致性。

Abstract: We study single-loop gradient-flow dynamics for nested optimization, where the outer variable evolves while auxiliary variables track the inner solution map. While existing analyses typically rely on problem- and condition-specific Lyapunov constructions, we propose, to our knowledge, the first unified Lyapunov template for continuous-time analysis that covers minimax, bilevel via a lifted penalty formulation, and min--min--max. Our proof is modular, built from reusable lemmas that yield a unified characterization of time-scale separation. This characterization bridges regimes from strong convexity/concavity to mere convexity through an error-bound condition, and produces explicit closed-form thresholds that avoid the coupled ratio conditions common in discrete-time analyses. We further compare the penalty dynamics with the ideal hyper-gradient flow, derive a finite-time tracking bound, and discuss an Euler one-step analogue; hypercleaning diagnostics show that the predicted relative time-scale regions remain visible under stable forward-Euler discretization.

</details>


### [79] [No-regret optimization of time-varying bilevel problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20962)
*Eliabelle Mauduit,Eloïse Berthier,Andrea Simonetto*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出W-SparQ-BL，一种贝叶斯优化框架，用于解决下层响应未知且随时间变化、仅能通过噪声零阶观测获取的双层优化问题。该框架采用多输出高斯过程建模下层映射，利用稀疏观测基近似控制噪声与时间变化的影响，并在平稳和时变场景下实现次线性动态遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层优化问题在博弈论等应用中广泛存在，但即使线性双层规划也是强NP难的。现有方法难以处理下层响应未知、仅能通过噪声零阶观测获取且可能随时间变化的场景，需要一种高效的优化框架来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出W-SparQ-BL框架：1) 使用多输出高斯过程建模未知的下层映射；2) 引入稀疏观测基近似以控制噪声和时变性的影响；3) 在贝叶斯优化框架下实现不确定性感知的高效优化。

Result: 建立了常见核函数（Matérn和平方指数核）下下层响应与标准RKHS假设之间的正则性联系；证明了W-SparQ-BL在平稳和时变场景下均能达到次线性动态遗憾；在代表性时变博弈问题上的实验验证了有效性。

Conclusion: W-SparQ-BL为未知且时变的下层响应双层优化问题提供了一种理论上保证次线性遗憾、实践上有效的贝叶斯优化解决方案。

Abstract: Bilevel optimization problems arise in many applications where decisions must account for the optimal response of another system, such as in game-theoretic settings. However, these problems are notoriously challenging, as even linear bilevel programs are strongly NP-hard. In this work, we consider bilevel optimization with a known upper-level objective and an unknown, potentially time-varying lower-level response, accessible only through noisy zeroth-order observations. We propose W-SparQ-BL, a Bayesian optimization framework that models the lower-level mapping using multi-output Gaussian processes and enables efficient optimization under uncertainty. Our approach leverages a sparse, observation-based approximation to control the effect of noise and temporal variability, while requiring only limited access to additional information over time. We establish regularity results linking the lower-level response to standard RKHS assumptions for common kernels, including Matérn and squared exponential. We prove that W-SparQ-BL achieves sublinear dynamic regret in both stationary and time-varying settings. Experiments on representative time-varying game-theoretic problems demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [80] [Scaled Graph Bounding Techniques for Reset Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21119)
*Timo de Groot,Maurice Heemels,Tom Oomen,Sebastiaan van den Eijnden*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对复位系统，利用二次耗散性与缩放图之间的联系，提出了基于分段二次存储函数来上界逼近复位系统缩放图的方法，并通过特定采样技术揭示了基于二次耗散性的通用缩放图近似方法存在根本性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复位系统可以克服线性时不变控制中的基本局限性，新提出的缩放（相对）图为复位系统提供了图形化分析与设计工具框架，类似于线性系统中广泛采用的环路成形方法。本文旨在推导复位系统缩放图的上界逼近技术，并获取对其精度的深入理解。

Method: 利用二次耗散性与缩放图之间的联系，将上界逼近问题重新表述为寻找分段二次存储函数；采用特定采样技术进行分析。

Result: 通过特定采样技术，揭示了基于二次耗散性的通用缩放图近似方法存在根本性局限。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了基于二次耗散性的缩放图近似方法的内在局限性，为复位系统的图形化分析工具设计提供了重要理论边界。

Abstract: Reset systems can overcome fundamental limitations of linear time-invariant control. The recently introduced notion of scaled (relative) graphs provides a promising framework for developing graphical analysis and design tools for reset systems, in line with widely adopted loopshaping methods for linear systems. The aim of this paper is to derive techniques for over-bounding the scaled graph of reset systems, and obtain insights in their accuracy. We exploit connections between quadratic dissipativity and scaled graphs to recast the over-bounding problem as the search for piecewise quadratic storage functions. Using specific sampling techniques, we reveal a fundamental limitation of general scaled graph approximation methods that are based on quadratic dissipativity.

</details>


### [81] [Beyond Nonlinear Small-Gain Design: DADS with Partial-State Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21344)
*Iasson Karafyllis,Miroslav Krstic*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将Eduardo Sontag提出的输入-输出稳定性(IOS)概念与死区自适应扰动抑制(DADS)控制方案相结合，研究了一类标量ODE与可能无限维PDE互联系统的部分状态调节问题。提出的DADS控制器无需小增益定理假设，不依赖扰动/参数界信息，成功应用于热PDE、输运PDE和含粘性阻尼的波PDE三种互联情形，均实现鲁棒调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 庆祝Eduardo Sontag的学术贡献，将DADS控制方案与针对PDE系统扩展的IOS理论相结合，解决ODE-PDE互联系统的部分状态调节问题，突破传统小增益定理对部分状态反馈的限制。

Method: 提出部分状态DADS控制器的设计流程，该控制器无需知道任何外部扰动或参数的界，通过死区自适应机制抑制扰动，避免对小增益定理的依赖。

Result: 该DADS控制器成功应用于三种不同PDE互联系统（热方程、输运方程、含粘性阻尼的波动方程），在几乎所有PDE未知的情况下均实现鲁棒调节。

Conclusion: 同一DADS控制器可在多种ODE-PDE互联结构中实现鲁棒部分状态调节，无需扰动/参数界先验知识，为无穷维系统的鲁棒自适应控制提供了新途径。

Abstract: Eduardo Sontag and coauthors studied Input-to-Output Stability (IOS) and the output asymptotic gain property. These notions changed control theory and recently had an impact on robust adaptive control through the Deadzone-Adapted Disturbance Suppression (DADS) control scheme. Moreover, recently the notion of IOS was extended to systems described by Partial Differential Equations (PDEs). In this work, we celebrate Eduardo Sontag by combining DADS and IOS for PDEs: we study the partial-state regulation problem for a scalar Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) which is interconnected with a possibly infinite-dimensional system. In such a case the DADS control scheme can allow an escape from the requirements of the small-gain theorem that is mainly used for partial-state feedback. We show the design procedure of partial-state DADS controllers and we prove robust regulation even in the presence of external inputs (disturbances) without assuming knowledge of any disturbance/parameter bounds. The DADS controller is applied to three different cases of the interconnection of an ODE with an almost completely unknown: (a) heat PDE, (b) transport PDE, and (c) wave PDE with viscous damping. We show that the same DADS controller can achieve robust regulation in all three cases.

</details>

### [82] [GraphDiffMed: Knowledge-Constrained Differential Attention with Pharmacological Graph Priors for Medication Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20188)
*Krati Saxena,Tomohiro Shibata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphDiffMed提出了一种基于双尺度差分注意力机制的药物推荐框架，通过分别在就诊内和就诊间过滤噪声信号，并结合药理约束，在MIMIC-III数据集上实现了更优的推荐质量和安全性平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么擅长时序建模，要么擅长药理知识整合（如DDI），但很少能同时兼顾二者并有效抑制电子健康记录中长序列、噪声和临床异质性带来的干扰。

Method: 提出GraphDiffMed框架，核心是双尺度差分注意力v2（Differential Attention v2），在就诊内和就诊间两个层级应用差分注意力机制来过滤虚假信号；同时在学习过程中融入药理知识约束（如药物相互作用）。

Result: 在MIMIC-III数据集上，GraphDiffMed在推荐质量和排序指标上持续优于强基线，并实现了更有利的安全性能平衡。消融实验表明该设计各部分均有效，且最佳配置仅使用了人口统计学辅助特征。

Conclusion: 将噪声感知的注意力机制与药理约束相结合，能够生成更可靠且更具临床意义的药物推荐方案。

Abstract: Recommending safe and effective medication combinations from electronic health records (EHRs) is a core clinical AI problem, yet it remains difficult because patient trajectories are long, noisy, and clinically heterogeneous. Existing methods typically excel at either temporal modeling across visits or pharmacological knowledge integration (e.g., drug-drug interactions, DDIs), but rarely achieve both while robustly suppressing noise. We present GraphDiffMed, a knowledge-constrained medication recommendation framework built on dual-scale Differential Attention v2. Differential attention is applied at both intra-visit and inter-visit levels to filter spurious signals within encounters and across longitudinal history, while pharmacological constraints are incorporated during learning. Experiments on MIMIC-III and ablation studies show that this design consistently improves recommendation quality and ranking over strong baselines while achieving a more favorable safety performance balance. We further find that the strongest-performing configuration uses only demographic auxiliary features under our experimental setting. Overall, GraphDiffMed demonstrates that combining noise-aware attention with pharmacological constraints yields more reliable and clinically meaningful medication recommendation. We open-source our code at https://github.com/saxenakrati09/GraphDiffMed.

</details>


### [83] [TabPFN-MT: A Natively Multitask In-Context Learner for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20234)
*Cormac Cureton,Narges Armanfard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN-MT是一种基于Prior-Data Fitted Networks的多任务扩展模型，通过扩展y编码器和共享解码器实现多目标上下文学习与同步推理，在<1000样本的小到中型数据集上达到SOTA，并将推理成本从O(T)降至O(1)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PFN仅支持单任务推理，预测多个目标值需多次前向调用且无法共享任务间信息，导致计算效率低下且缺乏跨任务依赖建模能力。

Method: 在扩展的多目标合成先验上训练，引入扩维y编码器和共享解码器头，支持多任务上下文学习与同步推理，无需梯度训练，完全依赖上下文学习。

Result: 在344个数据集（平均样本<1000）上，TabPFN-MT在深度表格多任务学习中达到SOTA；在多任务数据集中取得4.89的总体Accuracy排名（所有模型中最高平均排名）；同时保持与单任务集成相当的竞争力，并将T个任务的推理成本从O(T)降至O(1)。

Conclusion: TabPFN-MT通过上下文学习而非梯度训练，在小到中型数据集上实现了高效的多任务表格学习，大幅提升计算效率的同时保持了优异的预测性能。

Abstract: Prior-Data Fitted networks (PFNs) have been very successful in tabular contexts, handling prediction tasks in context. However, they are designed for single-task inference, meaning that predicting several target values within a context requires repeated forward calls and precludes inter-task information sharing. We propose TabPFN-MT, which is trained on an expanded multi-target synthetic prior to capture inter-task dependencies in context. This model uses an expanded $y$-encoder and a shared decoder head to enable multitask in-context learning and simultaneous inference. The model is uniquely specialized for small-to-medium datasets by relying on in-context learning rather than traditional gradient-based training. Within this regime (averaging fewer than 1,000 samples), extensive evaluations across 344 datasets demonstrate that TabPFN-MT establishes a new state-of-the-art for deep tabular multitask learning. Furthermore, despite the inherent compute asymmetry of joint optimization, our model remains highly competitive with the latest state-of-the-art single-task ensembles. Notably, on multitask datasets it achieves an overall Accuracy rank of 4.89, the highest average rank among all models tested. Crucially, TabPFN-MT delivers this highly competitive performance while reducing the inference cost for $T$ tasks from $O(T)$ to $O(1)$ forward passes, offering a massive computational efficiency improvement for multi-target tabular applications.

</details>


### [84] [Provably Learning Diffusion Models under the Manifold Hypothesis: Collapse and Refine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20235)
*Wei Huang,Andi Han,Mingyuan Bai,Huanjian Zhou,Qixin Zhang,Taiji Suzuki,Kenji Fukumizu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分数几何的折叠-细化机制，证明了扩散模型可以通过单目标分数匹配同时学习数据流形和密度分布，并据此设计了SiLD框架，在理论上将样本复杂度从环境维度降低到内在维度，实验上在多个数据集上匹配或超越VAE潜扩散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在生成高维数据方面效果显著，但理论上为何其训练能高效学习分数函数、绕过维度灾难（当数据支持在低维流形上时）尚未得到解释。现有潜扩散模型依赖启发式的KL正则化，缺乏理论保证。

Method: 识别出分数函数几何驱动的折叠-细化机制：小噪声尺度下，分数的发散奇异性驱动去噪映射快速维度折叠到数据流形投影；中等噪声尺度下，训练细化学习流形上的内在密度。基于此设计SiLD框架，将流形学习和密度估计统一于单一去噪分数匹配目标，替代VAE潜扩散模型的KL正则化。

Result: 理论上证明样本复杂度取决于内在维度而非环境维度。实验上在Stacked MNIST、CelebA变体以及分子生成基准上，SiLD在生成质量上匹配或超越VAE潜扩散模型，且一致地改善了重构性能。

Conclusion: 扩散模型的训练效率可以通过分数函数的几何性质（折叠-细化机制）得到理论解释，且SiLD框架验证了这一理论预测，为潜扩散模型提供了新的理论基础和实用方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models generate high-dimensional data with remarkable quality, yet how their training efficiently learns the score function, bypassing the curse of dimensionality when data is supported on low-dimensional manifolds, remains theoretically unexplained. We identify a collapse-and-refine mechanism driven by the geometry of the score function itself: at small noise scales, the diverging singularity of the score drives a rapid dimensional collapse of the induced denoising map onto the data manifold projection; at moderate noise scales, training refines the intrinsic density on the learned manifold. We instantiate this principle as Score-induced Latent Diffusion (SiLD), a two-stage framework in which both manifold learning and density estimation emerge from a single denoising score matching objective, replacing the heuristic KL regularization of VAE-based latent diffusion models. We prove that the resulting sample complexity depends on the intrinsic dimension rather than the ambient dimension. Experiments on Stacked MNIST, CelebA variants, and molecular generation benchmarks show that SiLD matches or outperforms VAE-based LDMs in generation quality and consistently improves reconstruction, validating our theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [85] [MagBridge-Battery: A Synthetic Bridge Dataset for Li-ion Magnetometry and State-of-Health Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20240)
*Sakthi Prabhu Gunasekar,Prasanna Kumar Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MagBridge-Battery v1.0 是一个合成的电池磁场数据集（含 6,760 个磁信号样本），它将真实磁形态与 SOH 标签桥接，为磁感应电池诊断提供了首个公开基准，包含 SOH 回归、二次寿命分类和异常检测三个主要任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电池健康诊断主要依赖电化学信号，磁感应技术虽能补充遗漏信息，但缺乏公开的电池磁场测量数据集与退化标签配对，限制了该方法的发展。

Method: 将 Mohammadi-Jerschow OSF 的真实磁形态与 PulseBat 数据集的 SOH 标签桥接，生成 6,760 个合成磁场信号。数据集包含三大子集：5,600 个基于 PulseBat 条件的接地样本、600 个合成传感器异常样本、560 个低电压 Regime-B 外推样本。采用无细胞重叠、无跨分割父子对、无样本 ID 重叠的主基准分割，定义了 SOH 回归、二次寿命分类、异常检测及异常子类型分类四项任务。

Result: SOH 回归达到 R²≈0.77，标签洗牌消融后 R²降至≈0，证实桥接编码了有效的 SOH 信息而非伪影。数据集在 Zenodo 发布（CC-BY-4.0），代码和基准套件在 Apache-2.0 下发布。

Conclusion: 该工作为磁感应电池诊断提供了首个公开基准，弥补了配对磁-电化学测量数据稀缺的空白。

Abstract: Battery health diagnostics today rely overwhelmingly on electrochemical signals measured at the cell terminals. A parallel literature has shown that magnetic sensing can resolve information that terminal-only measurements miss, but method development is limited by the absence, to the best of our knowledge, of public battery magnetic-measurement datasets paired with degradation labels. We release MagBridge-Battery v1.0, a synthetic dataset of 6,760 magnetic-field signatures that bridges real magnetic morphology from the Mohammadi-Jerschow Open Science Framework (OSF) archive with state-of-health (SOH) labels from the PulseBat dataset. The release contains 5,600 PulseBat-conditioned grounded samples, 600 synthetic sensor-anomaly samples derived from clean parents, and 560 low-voltage Regime-B extrapolation samples. A cell-disjoint, parent-child-leakage-free primary benchmark split is verified to contain zero overlapping cells, zero cross-split parent-child pairs, and zero sample-ID overlap. We define three primary benchmark tasks: SOH regression, second-life classification, and anomaly detection, plus an auxiliary anomaly-subtype classification task. A controlled label-shuffle ablation collapses SOH regression from R^2 approximately 0.77 to approximately 0, confirming that the bridge encodes input SOH non-trivially rather than producing label-aligned artifacts. The dataset is released on Zenodo under CC-BY-4.0, and the bridge code and benchmark suite are released under Apache-2.0. This work provides a public benchmark for magnetic-sensing battery diagnostics while paired magnetic-electrochemical measurements remain scarce.

</details>


### [86] [Geometry-Lite: Interpretable Safety Probing via Layer-Wise Margin Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20241)
*Woo Seob Sim,Yu Rang Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Geometry-Lite，一种轻量级提示级安全探针，通过分析多层几何特征（边界位置、层间变化、形状）来解构大语言模型的安全信号，发现安全证据主要依赖于持久性的边界位置几何（最终/极端边界和安全侧层占用），而非层间运动信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示级安全探针虽能有效检测不安全提示，但其多层几何结构——安全证据如何跨层形成、哪些几何特性支持低假阳性决策、以及基准迁移下的几何偏差——尚未得到清晰解释。

Method: Geometry-Lite将每层最终提示令牌表征映射为质心、局部邻域和有监督线性边界下的带符号边界，然后通过边界位置、层间变化和粗略形状三个维度汇总边界分布。在9个指令微调骨干模型（1.2B-70B）和7个安全基准上评估。

Result: 持久性边界位置几何（最终/极端边界与不安全侧层占用）主导了聚合检测性能；有限差分漂移和结构性摘要对AUROC贡献有限，但在低FPR阈值下可提供小幅度面向召回的修正；基准迁移下，优化线性边界在训练混合上表现尖锐，而类条件均值几何在困难保留子集上更可靠。

Conclusion: 提示级安全证据本质上不是层间运动信号，而是一种持久性的多层边界几何；其有用成分和读取级偏差在决策关键阈值下才会显现。

Abstract: Prompt-level safety probes for large language models use hidden-state representations to separate safe from unsafe prompts, but strong average detection performance does not explain the geometry of this separation. In particular, it remains unclear how safety evidence is formed across layers, which aspects of that layer-wise geometry support low-false-positive decisions, and which geometric biases remain stable under benchmark shift. We study this as an empirical decomposition problem and introduce Geometry-Lite, a compact prompt-level probe that maps each layer's final prompt-token representation to signed margins under centroid, local-neighborhood, and supervised linear-boundary readouts, then summarizes the resulting margin profiles by boundary position, layer-to-layer change, and coarse shape. Across nine instruction-tuned backbones ($1.2$B--$70$B) and seven safety benchmarks, Geometry-Lite improves over single-layer probes while remaining close to raw multi-layer score stacking, making it a useful instrument for analyzing the multi-layer safety signal. The decomposition shows that safety evidence is expressed primarily through persistent boundary-position geometry: final or extremal margins and unsafe-side layer occupancy dominate aggregate detection performance. In contrast, finite-difference drift and structural summaries add little to pooled AUROC, although drift can provide small recall-oriented corrections under shifted low-FPR thresholds. Under benchmark shift, optimized linear boundaries are sharp on the training mixture, whereas class-conditional mean geometry retains separation more reliably on a predefined hard held-out subset. Overall, prompt-level safety evidence is not primarily a layer-to-layer motion signal, but a persistent layer-wise margin geometry whose useful components and readout-level biases become visible in decision-critical regimes.

</details>


### [87] [LEAP: A closed-loop framework for perovskite precursor additive discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20242)
*Xin-De Wang,Zhi-Rui Chen,Ze-Feng Gao,Peng-Jie Guo,Cheng Mu,Zhong-Yi Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了LEAP框架，将领域专用的大语言模型与主动学习相结合，通过文献驱动的机制描述符和贝叶斯优化迭代筛选钙钛矿前驱体添加剂，实验验证了该方法能有效提升器件性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钙钛矿太阳能电池前驱体添加剂的发现依赖试错筛选效率低，化学空间庞大，亟需高效的智能筛选方法。

Method: 构建领域专用LLM提取添加剂文献中的机制知识，生成可解释描述符；结合贝叶斯优化进行不确定性感知的主动学习优先级排序；采用专家在环的迭代闭环框架。

Result: 领域专用模型在机制一致性推理上优于通用模型；三轮筛选后，6-CDQ和2-CNA处理的器件平均PCE分别达20.13%和20.87%，优于对照组的19.25%，冠军PCE为21.32%。

Conclusion: 文献驱动的机制描述符结合贝叶斯优化与专家可行性评估，能够实现机制感知的添加剂优先级排序，为钙钛矿光伏添加剂的高效发现提供了初步证据。

Abstract: Efficient discovery of precursor additives is essential for improving the performance of perovskite solar cells, yet the large chemical space makes conventional trial-and-error screening inefficient. We develop LEAP(LLM-driven Exploration via Active Learning for Perovskites), an expert-in-the-loop closed framework that couples a domain-specialized large language model(LLM) with active learning for iterative additive prioritization. The LLM is trained to extract mechanism-relevant knowledge from the perovskite additive literature and to represent candidate molecules through interpretable descriptors, which are further integrated into a Bayesian optimization workflow for uncertainty-aware prioritization under low-data conditions. Benchmark results on unseen literature show that the domain-specialized model outperforms general-purpose models in mechanism-consistent reasoning. Experimental validation in an expert-in-the-loop proof-of-concept study suggests improved additive prioritization across three screening rounds, leading to average device PCEs of 20.13% and 20.87% for the later-round 6-CDQ- and 2-CNA-treated devices, respectively, compared with 19.25% for the control, with a champion PCE of 21.32%. These results provide preliminary evidence that literature-grounded mechanistic descriptors, when coupled with Bayesian optimization and expert feasibility review, can support mechanism-aware additive prioritization in perovskite photovoltaics.

</details>


### [88] [GROW: Aligning GRPO with State-Action Modeling for Open-World VLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20246)
*Xiongbin Wu,Zhihao Luo,Shanzhe Lei,Lechao Zhang,Xuhong Wang,Jie Yang,Zhonglong Zheng,Yuanjie Zheng,Xin Tan,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了GROW框架，通过将完整轨迹分解为状态-动作样本并计算样本间优势，使得GRPO算法能有效应用于开放世界VLM智能体的多轮强化学习，在Minecraft任务中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM智能体主要依赖监督微调（SFT）和专家演示，而先进的强化学习算法GRPO因需要完整轨迹作为训练样本（导致上下文过长和噪声）而无法有效用于多轮强化学习任务。

Method: 提出GROW框架：将收集的轨迹分解为状态-动作样本，在样本间而非完整轨迹间计算优势；并提供替代分析证明该目标在简化假设下能保留GRPO的核心相对策略优化信号。

Result: 在800多个Minecraft任务上的实验表明，该方法达到了SOTA性能。

Conclusion: GROW框架有效解决了GRPO在多轮强化学习中的应用限制，证明了所提出的RL框架对开放世界VLM智能体的有效性。

Abstract: Recently, vision-language model (VLM) agents have shown promising progress in open-world tasks, where successful task completion often requires multiple turns of visual perception and action execution. However, existing methods still rely primarily on Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) with expert demonstrations, while the advanced reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm, specifically Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), has not been effectively employed for multi-turn RL in these tasks because standard GRPO requires full trajectories as training samples which leads to excessively long context and noise. To address this issue, we propose GROW, a RL framework for open-world VLM agents that decomposes collected trajectories into state-action samples, and computes advantages between these samples rather than treating a full trajectory as a single entity. We further provide a surrogate analysis indicating that, even though the grouped samples are conditioned on different local states rather than an identical prompt context, the objective can preserve the core relative policy optimization signal of GRPO under simplifying assumptions. Experiments on more than 800 Minecraft tasks show that our method achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance, demonstrating the effectiveness of our proposed RL framework for open-world VLM agents.

</details>


### [89] [CP-MoE: Consistency-Preserving Mixture-of-Experts for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20247)
*Yang Liu,Toan Nguyen,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CP-MoE通过引入瞬态专家（transient expert）来捕捉早期任务特定更新，并引导其与稳定专家集成，解决了MoE架构中持续学习时知识迁移与灾难性遗忘之间的权衡问题。在语言和视觉-语言基准测试上达到了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LoRA的MoE持续学习方法存在根本性权衡：要么过于激进地隔离专家，限制跨任务知识迁移；要么允许任务特定更新覆盖重要参数，导致严重遗忘。

Method: CP-MoE框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 一致性保持路由偏置（consistency-preserving routing bias），利用瞬态专家估计与稳定专家的表征相似性，引导路由选择更兼容的专家；(2) 瞬态专家引导的正则化机制（transient expert-guided regularisation mechanism），在合并过程中选择性保护重要的历史参数。

Result: 在SuperNI基准（语言任务）上达到SOTA性能，展现出更强的零样本迁移能力；在VQA v2数据集（多模态视觉推理）上有效扩展，持续减少遗忘，优于强MoE基线。

Conclusion: CP-MoE通过瞬态专家设计有效减少了参数干扰和遗忘，同时保留了跨任务知识迁移能力，在单模态和多模态持续学习场景中均验证了有效性。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting remains a major obstacle to continual learning in large language models (LLMs) and vision--language models (VLMs). Although Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures offer an efficient path to scaling, existing LoRA-based MoE continual learning methods still face a fundamental trade-off: they either isolate experts too aggressively, limiting knowledge transfer across tasks, or allow task-specific updates to overwrite important existing parameters, leading to severe forgetting. To address this, we propose CP-MoE, a continual learning framework built around a transient expert that captures early task-specific updates and guides their integration into stable experts. CP-MoE introduces a consistency-preserving routing bias, which uses the transient expert to estimate representation similarity with stable experts and steer routing towards more compatible expert selection, and a transient expert-guided regularisation mechanism, which selectively protects important historical parameters during merging. Together, these components reduce parameter interference and forgetting while preserving cross-task knowledge transfer. We validate CP-MoE on both unimodal and multimodal continual learning benchmarks with LLM-based and VLM-based MoE models. On SuperNI benchmark, spanning diverse sequential language tasks, CP-MoE achieves state-of-the-art performance and stronger zero-shot transfer to unseen tasks. On VQA v2 dataset, it scales effectively to multimodal visual reasoning, consistently reduces forgetting, and outperforms strong MoE baselines.

</details>


### [90] [Graph Transductive Sharpening: Leveraging Unlabeled Predictions in Node Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20248)
*Brown Zaz,Mar Gonzàlez I Català,Ferran Hernandez Caralt,Moshe Eliasof,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Transductive Sharpening（TS）方法，通过修改训练目标，在直推式半监督节点分类中，对未标注节点最小化预测熵、对标注节点平衡熵，从而在不改变骨干架构的情况下提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准监督目标函数丢弃了未标注节点的预测信号，而直推式模型在训练时会对所有节点（包括无标签节点）产生预测，这些预测可能包含有用的训练信息，但缺乏真实标签导致无法直接利用。

Method: 基于交叉熵可分解为标签相关的对齐项和标签无关的熵项这一观察，提出Transductive Sharpening（TS）：在损失层面，对未标注节点最小化预测熵，同时对标注节点进行反平衡，以提取未标注节点中的训练信号。

Result: 在多种节点分类基准数据集上，TS在不改变骨干架构的情况下取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 训练目标的设计是直推式半监督节点分类中一个有效的正交优化方向，预测置信度可作为无标签情况下提取训练信号的自然方式。

Abstract: In the transductive setting, where the full graph is observed but node labels are only partially available, progress in semi-supervised node classification has largely focused on architectural innovation. In this paper, we revisit an orthogonal axis: the training objective. We start from a simple observation: transductive models produce predictions for every node during training, including nodes without labels. These unlabeled-node predictions may contain useful training signal, but standard supervised objectives discard them because no ground-truth labels are available. Inspired by the decomposition of cross-entropy into a label-dependent alignment term and a label-independent entropy term, we propose prediction confidence as a natural way to extract this signal in the absence of labels. This motivates Transductive Sharpening (TS): a loss-level modification that minimizes prediction entropy on unlabeled nodes while counterbalancing this effect on labeled nodes. We evaluate Transductive Sharpening across a wide range of node-classification benchmarks and observe consistent performance improvements without requiring any changes to the backbone architecture. Code is available at https://github.com/transductive-sharpening/tunedGNN.

</details>


### [91] [Automated Kernel Discovery Towards Understanding High-dimensional Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20249)
*Taeyoung Yun,Woocheol Shin,Inhyuck Song,Jaewoo Lee,Jinkyoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Kernel Discovery的LLM驱动进化框架，用于高维贝叶斯优化中的核函数搜索，该方法通过两阶段LLM调用（先提出数学形式再转换为可执行代码）和LOO-CRPS选择准则，在5个高维BO基准上取得了平均排名1.2/17的优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯过程核函数设计在高维问题上依赖人工工程，自动化方法受限于核搜索空间（仅限基础核的加法和乘法），而基于LLM的方法需要以原始观测为条件，在高维下因上下文长度限制和模式提取困难而不可行。

Method: 采用LLM驱动的两阶段进化框架：第一阶段LLM提出新颖的数学形式，第二阶段将每个形式转换为可验证的可执行代码；同时提出留一法连续排序概率得分（LOO-CRPS）作为选择准则以惩罚过拟合的核函数。

Result: 在5个高维BO基准上，该方法在17个对比方法中平均排名1.2，优于所有竞争基线。

Conclusion: 该工作突破了传统核搜索空间的限制，无需以观测为条件，通过LLM驱动的方式自动发现有效的高维BO核函数，并分析了哪些核函数能带来性能提升。

Abstract: Gaussian Process (GP) kernels are central to Bayesian optimization (BO), yet designing effective kernels for high-dimensional problems still relies on extensive manual engineering. Existing automated approaches struggle in high dimensions for two bottlenecks: their kernel search space is limited to additions and multiplications of base kernels, and LLM-based approaches require conditioning on raw observations, which becomes infeasible due to context-length limits and the difficulty of extracting meaningful patterns. We introduce \textbf{Kernel Discovery}, a LLM-driven evolutionary framework for high-dimensional BO that searches a broader kernel space beyond predefined composition rules and does not require conditioning on observations. Motivated by the observation that directly prompting an LLM to generate kernel code yields syntactically varied but functionally identical kernels, we adopt a two-stage approach: an LLM first proposes novel mathematical forms, then a second LLM call converts each form into validated, executable code. We also propose a leave-one-out continuous ranked probability score (LOO-CRPS) as a selection criterion that penalizes overfitted kernels. On five high-dimensional BO benchmarks, our method achieves an average rank of \textbf{1.2 out of 17}, outperforming competitive baselines.
  We further analyze the discovered kernels to identify which kernels lead to improvements in high-dimensional BO.

</details>


### [92] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Safe Autonomous Driving Under Pedestrian Behavioral Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20255)
*Prakash Aryan,Kaushik Raghupathruni,Timo Kehrer,Sebastiano Panichella*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用多智能体强化学习（MARL）联合训练自动驾驶汽车（SDC）与12个行人，通过MAPPO算法让行人在Dijkstra路径规划基础上用RL策略控制等待/穿越决策，并引入隐藏的行人个性特征以模拟乱穿马路行为。相比固定策略基线，联合训练使SDC目标达成率从35%提升至78%，碰撞率从33%降至14%，且乱穿马路虽仅占穿越事件的13%却导致62%的碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于脚本或简化行人模型的仿真无法捕捉真实行人过街行为的异质性和不确定性，特别是受潜在个性特征驱动的乱穿马路行为，导致安全评估缺乏真实感。

Method: 构建MARL环境，SDC与12个行人使用MAPPO联合训练。行人底层运动采用Dijkstra路径规划，高层过街决策由RL策略控制，每个行人拥有随机采样的隐藏个性特征决定乱穿马路概率，SDC无法观测该特征。

Result: 联合训练的SDC在500回合评估中达成78%目标、14%碰撞率；最佳规则基线为35%目标、33%碰撞率。速度差异指标显示SDC在接近乱穿马路者时比接近使用人行横道者快2.65 m/s（0-3米范围内），表明SDC未能预判乱穿马路行为。乱穿马路占穿越事件13%但导致62%碰撞。MARL联合训练相比单智能体RL减少30%碰撞。

Conclusion: 联合训练行人产生更真实的交互场景，行人在SDC高速接近时学会等待，但隐藏个性特征导致的风险仍需进一步处理。乱穿马路是碰撞的主要来源，SDC需更好地识别和预判此类行为。

Abstract: Simulation-based testing of self-driving cars (SDCs) typically relies on scripted or simplified pedestrian models that do not capture the heterogeneity and uncertainty of real human crossing behavior. This limits the realism of safety assessments, especially in scenarios involving jaywalking, which is governed by latent personality traits that the vehicle cannot observe. We hypothesize that jointly training pedestrians and the SDC with multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) produces more realistic interaction scenarios than training the SDC against fixed pedestrian policies, and that the resulting behavior gap between predictable and unpredictable crossings can be measured directly from trajectories. This paper describes a MARL environment in which an SDC and 12 pedestrians are co-trained using Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (MAPPO). Pedestrian locomotion follows scripted Dijkstra pathfinding, while an RL policy controls high-level go/wait decisions. Jaywalking probability depends on a per-pedestrian personality trait sampled at episode start and hidden from the SDC. In 500-episode evaluations, the co-trained SDC reached 78% of goals with a 14% collision rate, compared to 35% goals and 33% collisions for the best rule-based baseline. A speed differential metric shows that the SDC traveled 2.65 m/s faster near jaywalkers than near crosswalk users at close range (0-3 m), indicating that jaywalking encounters were not anticipated. Jaywalking accounted for 13% of crossing events but was associated with 62% of collisions. Co-training with MARL pedestrians reduced collisions by 30% relative to single-agent RL, as pedestrians learned to wait when the SDC approached at speed.

</details>


### [93] [FBOS-RL: Feedback-Driven Bi-Objective Synergistic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20256)
*Xikai Zhang,Yongzhi Li,Likang Xiao,Yingze Zhang,Yanhua Cheng,Quan Chen,Peng Jiang,Wenjun Wu,Liu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FBOS-RL 提出反馈驱动的双目标协同强化学习框架，通过反馈引导的探索增强和两个相互增强的训练目标（利用导向的策略对齐 EPA 与探索导向的能力培养 ECC），解决了 GRPO 在任务超出模型当前能力时因缺乏高质量 rollout 导致训练停滞的问题，在同等 rollout 数量下显著提升训练效率和最终性能上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO 及其变体在训练中采用简单采样方案，所有 rollout 均基于同一原始 prompt 生成。当任务超出策略模型当前能力时，该方案极少能产生高质量 rollout，导致策略模型更新参数时缺乏有意义的梯度方向，训练陷入停滞。

Method: 提出 FBOS-RL 框架：(1) 反馈引导的探索增强（Feedback-Guided Exploration Enhancement），基于环境反馈引导模型探索；(2) 两个相互增强的训练目标：利用导向的策略对齐（EPA）和探索导向的能力培养（ECC），形成正向飞轮效应。

Result: 在同等 rollout 数量下，FBOS-RL 比 GRPO 和基于反馈的基线学习速度更快，最终达到更高性能上限，并且训练过程中保持更高的策略熵和更低的梯度范数。

Conclusion: EPA 和 ECC 能够相互增强，形成正向飞轮效应，显著提升强化学习的训练效率和最终性能天花板。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become a cornerstone for aligning and unlocking the reasoning capabilities of large-scale models. At its core, the training loop of GRPO and its variants alternates between rollout sampling and policy update. Unlike supervised learning, where each gradient step is anchored to an explicit ground-truth target, the optimal gradient direction for updating model parameters in this setting is not known a priori; the high-quality rollouts drawn during the sampling stage therefore act as the implicit "teacher" that guides every parameter update. However, GRPO adopt a simple sampling scheme that conditions all rollouts on the same original prompt. When a task lies beyond the policy model's current capability, this sampling scheme rarely yields a high-quality rollout, leaving the policy model without a meaningful gradient direction when updating its parameters, which causes training to stall. To address this issue, we propose FBOS-RL, a Feedback-Driven Bi-Objective Synergistic reinforcement learning framework. Specifically, we let the model perform Feedback-Guided Exploration Enhancement based on the feedback provided by the environment, and on top of this we design two mutually reinforcing training objectives: Exploitation-oriented Policy Alignment(EPA) and Exploration-oriented Capability Cultivation(ECC). Extensive experiments demonstrate that EPA and ECC can mutually reinforce each other, forming a positive flywheel effect that significantly improves both the training efficiency and the final performance ceiling of reinforcement learning. Specifically, under an identical number of rollouts, FBOS-RL learns substantially faster than GRPO and feedback-based baselines and ultimately attains a higher performance ceiling, while exhibiting higher policy entropy and lower gradient norms throughout training.

</details>


### [94] [Instance Discrimination for Link Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20257)
*Valentin Cuzin-Rambaud,Mathieu Lefort,Rémy Cazabet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对自监督学习中的链接预测任务，提出了基于链接表示而非节点表示的新模型L-GRACE和L-BGRL，并设计了基于社区结构的图增强方法，在无属性图上表现优异，达到与监督学习相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督学习方法在图领域主要聚焦于节点分类，对链接预测任务的研究较少；同时，图增强策略对链接预测性能的影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 1) 系统评估现有自监督模型在链接预测任务上的表现；2) 提出基于社区结构的图增强方法；3) 提出基于链接表示的新模型L-GRACE和L-BGRL，将对比学习从节点空间迁移到链接（边）表示空间。

Result: L-GRACE和L-BGRL在链接预测任务上优于现有自监督方法，尤其对无属性图提升显著；在监督和自监督场景下均达到与当前最优方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: 基于链接表示的自监督学习方法能够有效填补图自监督学习在链接预测任务上的空白，社区结构增强有助于提升链接预测性能。

Abstract: Recently, instance discrimination models have emerged as a major solution for self-supervised learning. Having already demonstrated its effectiveness in the image domain, instance discrimination learning is now proving equally convincing in the graph domain, in particular for node classification. However, fewer contributions have tackled the link prediction task. In this contribution, we propose to adapt existing methods to this context. We first provide a rigorous evaluation of existing self-supervised models in the field of link prediction, showing that the main performance depends on the augmentation process (like in computer vision). We then propose a new structural augmentation based on the community structure that is relevant for link prediction. Our main contribution introduces two new models, L-GRACE and L-BGRL, based on link representations instead of node representations, which improve the performance of the existing methods, especially on unattributed graphs, and we show that they perform on par with the state of the art, both in supervised and self-supervised contexts.

</details>


### [95] [A Differentiable Measure of Algebraic Complexity: Provably Exact Discovery of Group Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.23152)
*Dongsung Huh,Lior Horesh,Halyun Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Cayley表补全形式化离散代数规则发现，证明HyperCube张量分解的目标函数可分解为几何对齐（共线性）和逆ℓ2惩罚项，建立其与结合律和秩的等价关系，并推导出绝对下界H(Θ) ≥ H_inf(δ) ≥ 3|δ|，该下界当且仅当目标同构于群时达到，为可微度量精确刻画离散代数结构提供了存在性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从数据中发现离散代数规则是机器学习中的基本挑战，现有方法多依赖组合搜索，缺乏可微的连续度量来刻画离散代数结构的复杂性。

Method: 将问题形式化为Cayley表补全，以结合律违反程度替代线性秩作为复杂性度量；对HyperCube算子值张量分解进行严格景观分析，证明其目标函数可分解为共线性项和逆ℓ2惩罚项。

Result: 建立了共线性与结合律的等价关系，证明逆ℓ2惩罚在共线性流形上等价于逆秩惩罚；推导出绝对下界H(Θ) ≥ H_inf(δ) ≥ 3|δ|，且该下界当且仅当目标同构于群时达到；全局极小化子为群的正则表示（模酉规范）。

Conclusion: 证明了某些离散代数结构可以被可微度量精确刻画，使基于梯度的发现成为可能而无需组合搜索，解决了Huh (2025)的核心公开猜想，并在Lean 4中完成了机械验证。

Abstract: Discovering discrete algebraic rules from data is a fundamental challenge in machine learning. We formalize this problem through Cayley-table completion -- an algebraic counterpart to classical matrix completion -- where the degree of associativity violation replaces linear rank as the intrinsic measure of complexity. We provide a rigorous landscape analysis of HyperCube, an operator-valued tensor factorization, on the fully observed target table $δ$, proving that its global infimum $H_{\inf}(δ) := \inf_{Θ\in F_δ} H(Θ)$ implicitly defines an exact differentiable measure for this complexity. We show that HyperCube's native objective $H(Θ)$ decomposes into two components: geometric alignment (collinearity) and an inverse $\ell_2$ penalty. We establish that these continuous variational pressures induce core discrete properties: collinearity enforces associativity (Collinearity--Associativity Equivalence), and the inverse $\ell_2$ penalty reduces to an exact inverse rank penalty within the collinear manifold, driving the parameters toward full-rank unitarity. Consequently, we derive an absolute lower bound $H(Θ) \ge H_{\inf}(δ) \ge 3 \, |δ|$, where $|δ|$ is the target table size. We prove this absolute floor is attained if and only if the target is isotopic to a group, and characterize the global minimizer as the regular representation of the underlying group (up to unitary gauge), resolving the central open conjecture of Huh (2025). This work serves as an existence proof that certain discrete algebraic structures can be exactly characterized by differentiable measures, enabling gradient-based discovery without the need for combinatorial search. All theoretical results are mechanically verified in Lean 4 and confirmed via small-scale experiments.

</details>


### [96] [Take It or Leave It: Intent-Controlled Partial Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20030)
*Salil Parth Tripathi,Bertrand Chapron,Fabrice Collard,Nicolas Courty,Ronan Fablet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出意图控制的部分最优传输（IC-POT），将全局统一拒绝规则推广为逐点拒绝机制，允许根据侧信息、支撑几何或外部知识在每一点上决定是否匹配质量，并通过增广支撑上的平衡Kantorovich问题求解。在正-无标签学习、开放部分域适应及多模态卫星海洋测量中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最优传输要求严格匹配两个测度，部分最优传输虽允许不匹配质量，但采用全局统一的预算、标量回扣或拒绝规则，缺乏灵活性。实际应用中，不匹配决策往往依赖于特定点的可靠性、支撑几何或外部信息（如哪些成分应参与比较），亟需一种结构化的逐点拒绝机制。

Method: 将部分最优传输中的全局拒绝范式替换为两个测度上的逐点拒绝代价，构建IC-POT优化问题；证明其对偶形式可解释为局部接受阈值；通过将问题重铸为增广支撑上的平衡Kantorovich最优传输问题来求解。

Result: 在正-无标签学习、开放部分域适应任务中，融入编码统计结构的逐点拒绝规则优于固定基线流程；在多模态卫星海洋测量地球物理案例中，物理和传感器先验自然地指导拒绝机制，定义了可检索的可比信号信息。

Conclusion: IC-POT为部分最优传输提供了更具表达力的逐点拒绝框架，在需要侧信息指导匹配决策的应用场景中具有显著优势，理论分析与实际案例共同验证了其有效性。

Abstract: While optimal transport (OT) enforces a rigid constraint by requiring two measures to be matched exactly, partial optimal transport relaxes this requirement by allowing mass to remain unmatched through a global budget, scalar rebate, or uniform rejection rule. However, many applications call for more structured, pointwise rejection mechanisms, where the decision to leave mass unmatched depends on side-specific reliability, support geometry, or external information about which components should participate in the comparison. We introduce \emph{intent-controlled partial optimal transport} (IC-POT), a targeted generalization of partial transport that replaces the global rejection paradigm with pointwise rejection costs over both measures. We show that the resulting optimization problem admits a dual interpretation in terms of local acceptance thresholds and can be solved by recasting it as a balanced Kantorovich OT problem on an augmented support. Beyond theoretical analysis, we demonstrate the practical relevance of IC-POT in settings where rejection is driven by side information. In positive-unlabeled learning and open-partial domain adaptation, incorporating pointwise rejection rules that encode statistical structure improves fixed baseline pipelines. Finally, we motivate the use of IC-POT with a geophysical practical case: multi-modal satellite ocean measurements, for which physical and sensors priors naturally inform the rejection mechanism and define the retrieved comparable signal information.

</details>


### [97] [SURF: Steering the Scalarization Weight to Uniformly Traverse the Pareto Front](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20619)
*Liuyuan Jiang,Chentong Huang,Lisha Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SURF方法，通过几何分析解释标量化权重均匀采样导致帕累托前沿非均匀覆盖的原因，并利用弧长累积分布函数逆映射实现权重自适应选取，从而在帕累托前沿上产生均匀覆盖的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标优化中标量化方法广泛使用，但均匀采样标量化权重通常会导致帕累托前沿覆盖不均匀，现有方法缺乏对这一 mismatch 的几何解释和系统性解决方案。

Method: 通过标量化路径的几何分析，发现权重变化时对应解在帕累托前沿上的遍历速度非均匀；定义弧长累积分布函数(CDF)，通过逆CDF映射得到产生均匀覆盖的权重采样规则。对结构化问题（如双目标bandits）推导闭式解；对一般问题交替进行CDF重建和权重采样。

Result: 理论上证明在可证明条件下SURF线性收敛到不可避免的有限采样下界；实验上在bandits、多目标gymnasium和多目标LLM对齐任务中，SURF比基线方法更高效地实现了更均匀的帕累托前沿覆盖。

Conclusion: SURF通过几何驱动的权重自适应采样，有效解决了标量化中均匀权重导致帕累托前沿非均匀覆盖的问题，具有理论收敛保证和广泛的实证有效性。

Abstract: Scalarization is widely used in multi-objective optimization owing to its simplicity and scalability. In many applications, the goal is to generate solutions that represent diverse user preferences, ideally with uniform coverage of the Pareto front (PF). However, uniformly sampling scalarization weights usually induces non-uniform coverage of the PF. We explain this mismatch through a geometric analysis of the scalarization path. As the scalarization weight varies, the corresponding solutions trace the PF with a generally non-uniform traversal speed. This speed induces an arc-length cumulative distribution function (CDF); inverting this CDF map yields a principled rule for selecting weights that produce uniform PF coverage. Building on this insight, we propose SURF (Sampling Uniformly along the PaReto Front). For structured problems, including bi-objective bandits, we derive closed-form expressions for this CDF map and the resulting PF-aware weight sampling rule. For general problems, SURF alternates between CDF reconstruction and weight sampling. Theoretically, we show that under provable conditions, SURF converges linearly to an unavoidable finite-sampling floor. Empirically, experiments on bandits, multi-objective-gymnasium, and multi-objective LLM alignment demonstrate that SURF efficiently achieves more uniform PF coverage than baselines.

</details>


### [98] [Catching a Moving Subspace: Low-Rank Bandits Beyond Stationarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20269)
*Hamed Khosravi,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究分段平稳低秩线性上下文赌博机问题，提出了SPSC算法，在理论上刻画了可识别边界并实现了$\widetilde{O}(r\sqrt{T})$的动态遗憾，在11个基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作孤立处理两个事实：奖励存在于低维隐子空间且该子空间会发生漂移。平稳低秩赌博机利用秩结构但无法应对子空间变化，非平稳线性赌博机能适应漂移但需支付环境维度$d\sqrt{T}$的代价。本文旨在同时处理低秩与非平稳性，达到与内在秩相关的遗憾界。

Method: 提出了SPSC算法，交错使用各向同性探测与窗口化投影岭UCB在学习的$r$维子空间内进行利用；CUSUM变体在线发现分段边界。在理论上刻画了可识别边界的三个必要条件（已知噪声方差、有界状态-噪声耦合、满维探测支撑集）及其充分性。

Result: 动态遗憾为$\widetilde{O}(r\sqrt{T})+\widetilde{O}(T^{2/3})+O(W\,V_{\mathrm{in}})$，将环境维度$d\sqrt{T}$替换为内在秩$r$。在11个基准上，当$d-r\gtrsim T^{1/6}$时SPSC优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 这是首个在该设定下同时刻画可识别边界并达到内在秩动态遗憾率的工作。

Abstract: Many bandit deployments (recommendation, clinical dosing, ad targeting) share two facts prior work handles only in isolation: rewards live on a low-dimensional latent subspace, and that subspace drifts. Stationary low-rank bandits exploit rank but break under subspace change; non-stationary linear bandits adapt to drift but pay ambient rate $\widetilde{O}(d\sqrt{T})$. We study piecewise-stationary low-rank linear contextual bandits with scalar feedback: $θ_t = B_k^\star w_t$ with rank-$r$ factor $B_k^\star\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times r}$ constant within each of $K$ unknown segments and able to shift at boundaries. Our results are tight along three axes. (i) Identification boundary. With single-play scalar rewards, the moving subspace is recoverable through quadratic functionals of rewards iff three probe-side conditions hold: known noise variance, bounded state-noise coupling, and full-dimensional probe support. Each is necessary in the unrestricted-second-moment problem, and jointly they are sufficient, characterizing the boundary of the solvable region. (ii) Algorithm and dynamic regret. SPSC interleaves isotropic probes with windowed projected ridge-UCB exploitation inside the learned $r$-dimensional subspace; a CUSUM-style variant discovers segment boundaries online. The costed dynamic regret is $\widetilde{O}(r\sqrt{T})+\widetilde{O}(T^{2/3})+O(W\,V_{\mathrm{in}})$, replacing the ambient $d\sqrt{T}$ rate with the intrinsic rank. (iii) Empirics. On eleven benchmarks spanning synthetic, UCI/MovieLens, semi-synthetic clinical, and ZOZOTOWN production-log data, SPSC outperforms non-stationary and low-rank baselines whenever $d-r\gtrsim T^{1/6}$, matching the analytical crossover. To our knowledge, this is the first work to characterize the identification boundary and attain the intrinsic-rank dynamic-regret rate in this setting.

</details>


### [99] [Correcting Stochastic Update Bias in Preconditioned Language Model Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20756)
*Nikhil Nayak,Julia White,Urchade Zaratiana,Kelton Zhang,Henrijs Princis,Dhruv Atreja,Henry Fawcett,Matthew Thomas,George Hurn-Maloney,Ash Lewis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现预条件优化器（如AdamW、Sophia、Shampoo）在语言模型训练中存在两种有限样本偏差：梯度-预条件子耦合偏差和预条件子逆的非线性偏差。提出单批次偏差校正框架，通过交叉拟合预条件和方差校正逆估计消除偏差，在Qwen2.5-0.5B预训练中困惑度降低0.07-0.15 nats，指令微调效果中性或正向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预条件优化器广泛应用于语言模型训练，但其随机更新规则通常被视为种群预条件下降的直接近似，忽略了有限样本下的两种偏差：梯度与预条件子来自同一个小批量导致的耦合偏差，以及预条件子逆估计的非线性偏差。这些偏差未被系统研究或校正。

Method: 提出单批次偏差校正框架：1）交叉拟合预条件（Cross-fitted Preconditioning）：将小批量拆分为独立微批次组，分别估计梯度与预条件子，消除耦合偏差；2）方差校正逆估计（Variance-corrected Inversion）：利用微批次变异性减去delta方法的领先阶偏差项，校正非线性逆估计偏差。框架适配对角动量（AdamW）、对角曲率（Sophia）和矩阵预条件（Shampoo）方法。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-0.5B预训练上，偏差校正使AdamW、Sophia、Shampoo的保留集困惑度分别降低0.15、0.07和0.11 nats。在混合质量预训练和下游指令微调中效果持续中性至正向。

Conclusion: 偏差校正是减少有限样本更新偏差、提升预条件优化器性能的实用机制，可广泛适用于各类预条件优化方法。

Abstract: Preconditioned optimizers are central to language model training, but their stochastic update rules are usually treated as direct approximations to population preconditioned descent. We show that this view misses two finite-sample biases. First, the gradient and preconditioner are typically estimated from the same minibatch, introducing gradient--preconditioner coupling bias. Second, even when the preconditioner estimate is unbiased, its inverse or inverse-root is generally biased because inversion is nonlinear. We propose a single-batch bias-correction framework that addresses both effects: cross-fitted preconditioning estimates the numerator and preconditioner from independent microbatch groups, while variance-corrected inversion uses microbatch variability to subtract the leading delta-method bias term. The framework applies to diagonal moment, diagonal curvature, and matrix preconditioning methods, instantiated in AdamW, Sophia, and Shampoo. Bias correction reduces held-out pretraining loss on Qwen2.5-0.5B by $0.15$, $0.07$, and $0.11$ nats, respectively; the effects on mixed-quality pretraining and downstream instruction tuning are consistently neutral-to-positive. Together, these results establish bias correction as a practical mechanism for reducing finite-sample update bias and improving the performance of preconditioned optimizers.

</details>


### [100] [Conformal Selective Acting: Anytime-Valid Risk Control for RLVR-Trained LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20270)
*Hamed Khosravi,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CSA（Conformal Selective Acting）包装器，为使用RLVR微调本地专家LLM的受监管机构提供每轮部署的安全认证。CSA通过为每个阈值维护Ville型e-process，在Bonferroni网格上实现任意路径的选择性风险控制，证明风险界限R_T^act≤α+O(N_T^{-1/2})，并在八项专家基准、十六项对抗分布偏移和五项在线RLVR实验中，成为唯一同时满足路径有效性和非拒绝部署的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受监管机构部署经RLVR微调的本地专家LLM时，需要每轮（而非长期平均）提供安全认证，且不能跨部署池化。现有包装器无法满足这一需求：离线共形风险方法要求可交换性，在线共形方法仅约束长期平均，非可交换扩展仅边际有效，A-RCPS控制边际风险而非选择性风险。因此需要一种新的任意路径有效、控制选择性风险的部署侧包装器。

Method: 提出（检验统计量，有效性保证，部署规则）框架，识别出空白单元：每个阈值的e-process、选择性风险、任意路径有效性、最大认证阈值规则。CSA在每个轮次为Bonferroni网格上的每个阈值维护一个Ville型e-process，基于RLVR过滤评估。利用可预测更新和等渗校准的单调风险，证明任意路径选择性风险界限。

Result: 理论证明三个关键结果：(i) 任意路径选择性风险界限R_T^act≤α+O(N_T^{-1/2})，(ii) 速率最优认证匹配Θ(η̄^{-2} log(1/δ))，(iii) 与视界无关的释放率间隙。实验覆盖八项专家基准（480条流）、十六项对抗分布偏移单元（160条流）和五项在线Expert-Iteration RLVR实验（10,300轮，跨四个基础模型、三个架构家族），CSA是十种对比方法中唯一满足路径有效性和非拒绝部署的方法。

Conclusion: CSA是部署侧的补充性包装器，与模型正交，适用于无法使用前沿API的运营商。论文不提出新LLM、训练算法或策略类，而是解决受监管环境中每轮安全认证这一关键工程问题。

Abstract: A local specialist LLM, fine-tuned with reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) on operator-local data, is installed in a regulated organization with per-deployment error budget $α$. The operator needs a safety certificate for this deployment's stream at every round: no pooling across deployments, no waiting for a long-run average. Existing wrappers cannot deliver this on adaptive, online-updated streams: offline conformal-risk methods require exchangeability; online-conformal methods bound only long-run averages; non-exchangeable extensions are marginally valid; and the closest anytime wrapper, A-RCPS, controls marginal rather than selective risk. Using a (test statistic, validity guarantee, deployment rule) framework, we identify one empty cell forced by deployment requirements: e-process per threshold, selective risk, anytime-pathwise validity, max-certified-threshold rule. Conformal Selective Acting (CSA) fills it as a per-round wrapper maintaining a Ville-type e-process per threshold on a Bonferroni grid, evaluated against the RLVR filtration. Under predictable updates and isotonic-calibrated monotone risk we prove (i) an anytime-pathwise selective-risk bound $R_T^{\mathrm{act}}\leα+O(N_T^{-1/2})$, (ii) rate-optimal certification matching $Θ(\barη^{-2}\log(1/δ))$, and (iii) a horizon-independent release-rate gap. Across eight specialist benchmarks ($480$ streams), sixteen adversarial distribution-shift cells ($160$ streams), and five live Expert-Iteration RLVR cells with online LoRA over four base models in three architecture families ($10{,}300$ rounds), CSA is the only method among ten compared that satisfies pathwise validity and non-refusing deployment on every cell. We do not propose a new LLM, training algorithm, or policy class; CSA is the deployment-side complement, orthogonal to the model, for operators who cannot use a frontier API.

</details>


### [101] [LOSCAR-SGD: Local SGD with Communication-Computation Overlap and Delay-Corrected Sparse Model Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20866)
*Yassine Maziane,Ammar Mahran,Artavazd Maranjyan,Peter Richtárik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LOSCAR-SGD，一种结合通信压缩、本地训练和通信-计算重叠的分布式SGD方法，并首次给出三者组合的理论收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式学习中通信是主要瓶颈，现有方法缺乏同时结合通信压缩、本地训练和通信-计算重叠这三种策略的理论分析。

Method: 提出LOSCAR-SGD，采用稀疏通信（仅传输模型坐标的子集）、异质性本地步数，以及延迟校正合并规则来融合滞后的同步信息而不丢弃重叠阶段的计算进度。

Result: 给出了光滑非凸目标下的收敛保证，分析了稀疏度、重叠和工人异质性对收敛速率的影响；实验表明通信-计算重叠减少训练时间，延迟校正合并优于朴素覆盖策略。

Conclusion: LOSCAR-SGD是首个为同时结合通信压缩、本地训练和通信-计算重叠提供理论保证的方法，验证了各组件在实际中的有效性。

Abstract: Communication is a major bottleneck in distributed learning, especially in large-scale settings and in federated learning environments with slow links. Three standard ways to reduce this cost are communication compression, local training, and communication-computation overlap. Methods that combine these ingredients are used in practice and have been found to be effective for large-scale training, but there is little theory for methods that combine all three. We study a heterogeneous-compute setting in which different workers may take different numbers of local steps, and we propose LOSCAR-SGD, a Local SGD method that communicates only a sparse subset of model coordinates and continues optimizing while communication is in flight. A key ingredient is a delay-corrected merge rule that incorporates delayed synchronized information without discarding the progress made during the overlap phase. We give convergence guarantees for smooth non-convex objectives and show how sparsity, overlap, and worker heterogeneity affect the rate. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first theory for this combination of ingredients. Experiments further show that communication-computation overlap reduces training time and that the delay-corrected merge outperforms naive overwriting.

</details>


### [102] [Smaller Abstract State Spaces Enable Cross-Scale Generalization in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20272)
*Nasehatul Mustakim,Lucas Lehnert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个关于强化学习智能体如何在分布外（OOD）场景下实现泛化的理论模型，扩展了部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）中的状态抽象框架，并定义了后继加权模型约简方法，通过推导OOD测试性能的误差界，证明了将智能体约束在小规模有限抽象状态空间是实现任务泛化的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够轻松地将抽象概念泛化到更复杂或更大的任务中，而现有的强化学习系统缺乏这种OOD泛化能力，因此需要从理论上建立实现这种泛化的基础模型。

Method: 1）将现有状态抽象框架和证明技术扩展到POMDP；2）定义后继加权模型约简，一种允许压缩到更小抽象空间的变体；3）推导智能体OOD测试性能的误差界，将性能损失分解为近似误差和估计误差。

Result: 推导出OOD泛化可实现的边界条件，证明减少抽象状态空间大小能够提升测试性能和OOD泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将智能体约束在小的有限抽象状态集上操作是实现复杂任务泛化的必要条件，该结果激励了跨不同复杂度任务可扩展的RL架构的进一步研究。

Abstract: While humans readily generalize abstract concepts to more complex or larger tasks, building Reinforcement Learning (RL) systems with this ability remains elusive. Here, we present the first theoretical model of how such Out-of-Distribution (OOD) generalization can be achieved in RL agents. Our approach considers Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs) and assumes that an intelligent agent uses an abstraction function to determine which experiences can be treated as equivalent and which must be distinguished. First, we extend the existing state abstraction framework and proof techniques to POMDPs. Then, we define a successor-weighted model reduction, a model reduction variant that enables compression into smaller abstract spaces than prior definitions allow. We derive a bound on the agent's OOD test performance, thereby defining the conditions under which OOD generalization is achievable. This bound decomposes an agent's performance loss into approximation and estimation errors, revealing how reducing an agent's abstract state space size improves test performance and OOD generalization. Our analysis suggests that constraining an agent to operate over a small, finite set of abstract states is necessary for achieving generalization to more complex tasks. Our results motivate further research into learning RL architectures that scale across tasks of varying complexity levels.

</details>


### [103] [Beyond the Bellman Recursion: A Pontryagin-Guided Framework for Non-Exponential Discounting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20996)
*Hojin Ko,Jeonggyu Huh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出在非指数贴现（如双曲贴现、生存贴现）下，基于Bellman递归的值函数和Actor-Critic方法会结构性地失效，并提出Pontryagin-Guided Direct Policy Optimization (PG-DPO)，一种结合Pontryagin最大值原理与蒙特卡洛 rollout 的变分框架，通过Adjoint-MC投影实现逐点哈密顿量最大化，在基准任务中显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法依赖Bellman递归，而该递归仅在指数贴现下成立；非指数贴现（更符合人类偏好和生存过程）会破坏其结构，导致标准动态规划失效。

Method: 提出PG-DPO框架，放弃递归，将Pontryagin最大值原理与蒙特卡洛rollout结合，通过Adjoint-MC投影强制实现逐点哈密顿量最大化。

Result: 在多维双曲贴现和生存贴现基准上，PG-DPO的精度和稳定性均优于基于方程的求解器和基于critic的基线方法（后两者在任务中发散）。

Conclusion: PG-DPO有效解决了非指数贴现下强化学习方法的崩溃问题，为更符合人类偏好的贴现建模提供了可行的替代框架。

Abstract: Most value-based and actor--critic reinforcement learning methods rely on Bellman-style recursions, yet these recursions collapse under non-exponential discounting common in human preferences and survival processes. We show the breakdown is structural: exponential discounting sits at a fragile intersection of multiplicativity and time homogeneity, and violating either property breaks standard dynamic programming. To overcome this, we propose Pontryagin-Guided Direct Policy Optimization (PG-DPO), a variational framework that abandons recursion and couples the Pontryagin Maximum Principle with Monte Carlo rollouts via an Adjoint-MC projection enforcing pointwise Hamiltonian maximization. Across multi-dimensional hyperbolic and survival-discount benchmarks, PG-DPO improves accuracy and stability where equation-driven solvers and critic-based baselines diverge.

</details>


### [104] [OmniISR: A Unified Framework for Centralized and Federated Learning via Intermediate Supervision and Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20276)
*Wei-Bin Kou,Guangxu Zhu,Ming Tang,Chen Zhang,Lisheng Wu,Lei Zhou,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OmniISR统一框架，通过中间监督和正则化信号融合集中学习与联邦学习，实现跨异构法律框架的边缘智能部署，并提供严格理论收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘智能的全球部署面临异构法律框架：部分地区允许集中学习(CL)而另一些地区强制数据本地化需联邦学习(FL)。这两种优化范式存在根本性不兼容（CL的全局无偏梯度与内部协变量偏移 vs FL的有偏漂移局部更新），导致任何简单融合缺乏严格理论保证。

Method: 提出OmniISR框架，在多个隐藏层引入：(1) 互信息(MI)作为中间监督，对齐CL中的内部协变量偏移和FL中的客户端漂移表示；(2) 负熵(NE)作为中间正则化器，惩罚过度自信预测、保留表征不确定性、避免设备特定坍塌。支持纯CL、纯FL和混合CL-FL三种训练模式。

Result: 推导了统一的ISR无关非渐近O(1/√T)收敛界、ISR减少客户端漂移的联邦漂移界、梯度对齐保证以及加速逃离严格鞍点的逃逸时间界。实验表明OmniISR在集中和联邦范式下均持续提升模型性能，将CL-FL差距降低22.60%，在多个FL算法上取得37/48配对指标胜利。

Conclusion: OmniISR通过中间监督与正则化实现了CL与FL的统一融合，具备严格理论收敛保证，有效弥合了两种范式之间的性能差距。

Abstract: The global deployment of edge intelligence operates across heterogeneous legal frameworks. While some regions permit centralized learning (CL) via cloud data aggregation, others enforce strict data localization, necessitating federated learning (FL). This operational dichotomy introduces two incompatible optimization regimes (i.e., unbiased global gradients yet coupled with internal covariate shift in CL versus biased, drift-prone local updates in FL), resulting in that any naive integration of the two lacks rigorous theoretical guarantees. To fill this gap, we propose OmniISR, a unified framework that fuses pure CL, pure FL, and hybrid CL-FL training modes via equipping intermediate supervision and regularization (ISR) signals at multiple hidden layers. Specifically, we propose (i) to use mutual-information (MI) as intermediate supervision to align shifting internal covariate in CL and client-drifting representations in FL, and (ii) to adopt negative-entropy (NE) as intermediate regularizer to penalize overconfident prediction, preserve representational uncertainty, and avoid device-specific collapse. On the theory side, we derive (i) a unified, ISR-agnostic, and non-asymptotic O(1/sqrt(T)) convergence bound that shows the introduced ISR does not violate standard SGD convergence, (ii) a federated drift-bound that quantifies the ISR-reduced client drift, (iii) a gradient-alignment guarantee that ensures non-conflicting CL and FL updates under mild bias, and (iv) an explicit escape-time bound that indicates that CL-FL hybrid mixing enlarges effective stochasticity and accelerates escape from strict saddles. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OmniISR consistently improves model performance in both centralized and federated paradigms, reduces the CL-FL gap by 22.60%, and yields 37/48 paired metric wins across multiple FL algorithms.

</details>


### [105] [ClaimDiff-RL: Fine-Grained Caption Reinforcement Learning through Visual Claim Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20278)
*Tianle Li,Xuyang Shen,Yan Ma,Rongxin Guo,Shaoxiang Chen,Jiacheng Chen,Haochen Wang,Hongyang Tang,Yucong Zhou,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ClaimDiff-RL框架，以基于参考的原子性声明差异作为奖励单元进行标题强化学习，将幻觉和遗漏事实分别可测量和可调优，在细粒度指标上甚至超越Gemini-3-Pro-Preview。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长格式图像标题的RL中存在奖励粒度问题：标题作为完整序列被评判，而重要错误发生在单个视觉声明层面。整体标量奖励将局部错误压缩为单一序列级信号，模糊了事实性与覆盖度之间的权衡。

Method: 提出ClaimDiff-RL框架：给定图像、演员标题和参考标题，多模态判断器枚举视觉基础的差异，逐条验证差异，赋予开放词汇错误类型和严重级别，生成每条差异的统计数据用于奖励组成。

Result: 在160张人工标注的诊断基准、公开标题基准和VQA基准上，ClaimDiff-RL改善了幻觉-遗漏事实平衡，保持通用能力，在物体计数、空间关系、场景识别等细粒度能力维度上超越Gemini-3-Pro-Preview。

Conclusion: 类型化、可验证的声明差异是细粒度、可诊断的标题RL的有效奖励单元。

Abstract: Long-form image captioning exposes a reward granularity problem in RL: captions are judged as whole sequences, while the important errors occur at the level of individual visual claims. A good dense caption should be both faithful and informative, avoiding hallucination without omitting salient details. Yet pairwise preferences, reference-based metrics, and holistic scalar rewards compress these local errors into a single sequence-level signal, obscuring the tradeoff between factuality and coverage. We introduce ClaimDiff-RL, a framework that uses reference-conditioned atomic claim differences as the reward unit for caption RL. Given an image, an actor caption, and a reference caption, a multimodal judge enumerates visually grounded differences, verifies each difference against the image, assigns open-vocabulary error types and severity levels, and produces per-difference statistics for reward composition. This makes hallucinated claims and omitted salient facts separately measurable and tunable. Experiments show that holistic scalar rewards can reduce hallucination by increasing missing facts, while ClaimDiff-RL exposes this faithfulness and coverage tradeoff and enables more balanced operating points. On a 160-image human-labeled diagnostic benchmark, public captioning benchmarks, and VQA benchmarks, ClaimDiff-RL improves the hallucination--missing-fact balance, preserves general capability, and even surpasses Gemini-3-Pro-Preview on several fine-grained Capability dimensions such as object counting, spatial relations, and scene recognition. These results suggest that typed, verifiable claim differences are an effective reward unit for fine-grained and diagnosable caption RL.

</details>


### [106] [Introspective X Training: Feedback Conditioning Improves Scaling Across all LLM Training Stages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20285)
*Brandon Cui,Ximing Lu,Jaehun Jung,Syeda Nahida Akter,Hyunwoo Kim,Yuxiao Qu,David Acuna,Shrimai Prabhumoye,Yejin Choi,Prithviraj Ammanabrolu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Introspective Training (IXT)方法，通过反思奖励模型生成自然语言反馈来标注数据，实现从预训练早期阶段就进行质量感知训练，在7.5-12B参数、最高18T token的密集Transformer LLM上实现最高2.8倍计算效率提升，并在数学和代码领域达到传统方法无法企及的性能水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前LLM训练管线中不同阶段（如预训练、后训练）之间的动态信息无法有效传递的问题，探索如何利用后期训练阶段的动态信息来指导早期训练阶段，从而实现更高效的计算扩展。

Method: 提出Introspective Training (IXT)，受离线奖励条件强化学习启发。使用思考奖励模型（thinking reward model）为数据生成基于自然语言批评的反馈标注，通过将生成的反馈作为前缀条件（prefix-conditioning）来训练模型，确保从训练早期就开始差异化对待不同token。

Result: 在7.5-12B参数、最高18 Trillion tokens的密集Transformer LLM上，IXT方法实现了：1）弯折缩放曲线，最高达2.8倍计算效率提升；2）在数学和代码领域达到传统训练方法无法实现的性能水平。

Conclusion: IXT通过将后训练阶段的反刳性反馈信号引入预训练等早期阶段，有效打破了传统训练管线中不同阶段的信息壁垒，实现了更高效的计算扩展，尤其在数学和代码等复杂领域展现出显著优势。

Abstract: We tackle the question of how to scale more efficiently across the many, ever-growing stages of current LLM training pipelines. Our guiding intuition stems from the fact that the dynamics of later stages of the pipeline, e.g. post-training, can be used to inform earlier stages such as pre-training. To this end, we propose Introspective Training (or IXT), inspired by offline reward-conditioned reinforcement learning and applicable to any stage of training. IXT uses a thinking reward model to annotate data with natural language critique based feedback, enabling quality aware training from the earliest stages of the pipeline. Models are then trained by prefix-conditioning the data with the generated feedback -- ensuring that not all tokens are treated equally starting much earlier in training than usual. Comprehensive experiments on 7.5-12B transformer-based dense LLMs trained from scratch all the way up to 18 Trillion tokens seen show that our method: bends scaling curves resulting in up to 2.8x more compute efficiency generally; and reaches performance levels unachievable for models trained otherwise in domains such as math and code.

</details>


### [107] [FusionCell: Cross-Attentive Fusion of Layout Geometry and Netlist Topology for Standard-Cell Performance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20287)
*Haoyi Zhang,Kairong Guo,Bojie Zhang,Yibo Lin,Runsheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FusionCell是一个双模态预测器，融合了布局几何与网表拓扑信息，在7nm工艺数据集上实现了0.92%的平均MAPE，相比电路仿真加速了数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准单元的特征化依赖慢速仿真扫描，现有快速预测器忽略布局几何信息，无法捕捉耦合和布局依赖效应，需要联合表征布局几何和网表拓扑以实现精确性能预测。

Method: FusionCell采用双模态架构：DeiT编码器处理三层布线布局，图变压器建模异构器件/网图；通过拓扑引导机制，网表作为结构"地图"主动查询布局中相关物理区域，实现几何与拓扑的联合推理。
